  9779 | function shiftTimetableDate(value, amountDays) {
  9780 |   const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  9781 |   if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return dateKey(new Date());

  9785 | function timetableWeekLabel(value) {
  9786 |   const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  9787 |   if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return "주간 선택";
  9788 |   const day = base.getDay();
  9789 |   base.setDate(base.getDate() + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day));
  9790 |   const end = new Date(base); end.setDate(end.getDate() + 4);
  9791 |   const md = d => `${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}`;
  9792 |   return `${md(base)} ~ ${md(end)}`;
  9793 | }
